Result: 在TinyShakespeare上验证损失提升+0.103，Penn Treebank上+0.101，表明数据集无关性。消融实验表明固定小波基（Morlet、Daubechies）次优，最优能量方向是数据自适应的。学习到的能量阈值收敛至~0.35，对应英文文本中具有高于平均谱能量的token比例（~36%）。

Conclusion: 能量门控注意力是一种轻量有效的注意力改进方法，验证了token信息密度应指导注意力分配的假设；数据自适应的谱能量优于固定小波基，学习小波包是未来方向。

Original Abstract: Standard transformer attention computes pairwise similarity between queries and keys, treating all tokens as equally salient regardless of their intrinsic informational content. In turbulent fluid dynamics, coherent structures -- the energetically dominant, spatially organized patterns that persist amid background chaos -- carry a disproportionate fraction of total energy and govern all transport. We propose that tokens play an analogous role in transformer attention: informationally dense positions (morphological boundaries, syntactic heads, discourse markers) concentrate spectral energy and should attract proportionally more attention than background tokens (function words, repeated patterns, low-information filler). We propose Energy-Gated Attention (EGA): a simple modification that gates value aggregation by the spectral energy of key token embeddings, computed by a single learned linear projection that discovers the dominant spectral mode of the embedding field. On TinyShakespeare, EGA achieves +0.103 validation loss improvement with only 12,480 additional parameters (<0.26% overhead) and no measurable computational cost. The result is consistent on Penn Treebank (+0.101), demonstrating dataset independence. A systematic ablation across three wavelet families (fixed Morlet, Daubechies db2/db4, and a parametric Morlet) establishes that fixed structured bases are suboptimal -- the optimal energy direction is data-adaptive and non-sinusoidal -- while identifying learned wavelet packets as a promising open direction. The learned energy threshold converges to tau ~= 0.35 independently of initialization, corresponding to the fraction (~36%) of tokens carrying above-average spectral energy in English text, a stable linguistic property consistent with the fraction of content words in running English text.

中文摘要翻译: 标准的Transformer注意力机制计算查询和键之间的成对相似度，将所有token视为具有同等显著性，而忽略其内在信息内容的差异。在湍流流体动力学中，相干结构——在背景混沌中持续存在的能量主导、空间上有组织的模式——携带了不成比例的总能量并控制所有输运过程。我们提出，token在Transformer注意力中扮演类似角色：信息密集的位置（形态边界、句法核心、话语标记）集中了谱能量，应比背景token（功能词、重复模式、低信息填充词）获得更多注意力。我们提出能量门控注意力（EGA）：一种简单的修改，通过由单个可学习线性投影计算出的key token嵌入的谱能量来门控值聚合，该线性投影发现嵌入场的主导谱模态。在TinyShakespeare上，EGA仅增加12,480个额外参数（<0.26%开销）且无额外计算成本，实现了+0.103的验证损失改进。该结果在Penn Treebank上保持一致（+0.101），证明了数据集独立性。跨三个小波族（固定Morlet、Daubechies db2/db4、参数化Morlet）的系统消融实验表明，固定结构化基是次优的——最优能量方向是数据自适应且非正弦的——同时将学习小波包识别为一个有前景的开放方向。学习到的能量阈值收敛至τ ≈ 0.35，与初始化无关，对应于英文文本中携带高于平均谱能量的token比例（约36%），这是一个稳定的语言属性，与英文连续文本中实词的比例一致。

</details>


### [122] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量偏移下的置信度校准问题，提出了期望一致性条件（ECC）及其对应的期望一致性损失（ECL），实现了无需目标域标签的置信度校准，并在理论和实验上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法大多假设训练和测试数据独立同分布，在协变量偏移下效果受限；而现有的协变量偏移校准方法存在类别校准困难、依赖不稳定的重要性加权等问题。

Method: 推导了协变量偏移下置信度校准的充要条件——期望一致性条件；基于该条件提出期望一致性损失（ECL），一种无监督域适应损失；证明了ECL的计算复杂度与期望校准误差（ECE）相同，并设计了基于小批量的可训练方案。

Result: 在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上验证了该方法的有效性，理论上证明了ECL与ECE具有相同的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移并不必然导致置信度失校准，期望一致性条件为校准提供了更弱的要求；ECL损失兼容多种校准方式，且具有理论保障和实用训练方案。

Original Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 分类模型的置信度校准在安全关键决策场景中至关重要，并已受到广泛关注。通用的置信度校准方法假设训练数据和测试数据独立同分布，这限制了它们在协变量偏移下的有效性。现有的协变量偏移校准方法在类别级校准或标准校准方面存在困难，并且在密度比过大或无界时往往依赖于不稳定的重要性加权。鉴于上述局限性，本文重新审视了协变量偏移下的置信度校准问题。首先，我们推导了协变量偏移下置信度校准的一个充要条件，称为期望一致性条件，该条件揭示了协变量偏移并不必然导致置信度失校准，并为置信度校准提供了比全局协变量分布对齐更弱的要求。然后，利用期望一致性条件，本文提出了一种无监督域适应损失来校准目标域的置信度，称为期望一致性损失（ECL），该损失兼容标准校准、类别级校准和顶部标签校准。第三，我们证明了计算ECL损失与期望校准误差（ECE）具有相同的样本复杂度，并为ECL损失提供了有理论依据的小批量可训练方案。最后，我们在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Single Slot: Joint Optimization for Multi-Slot Guaranteed Display Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21556)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Jiaming Deng,Miao Xie,Linyou Cai,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向多广告位的联合优化框架，通过合同轮盘机制和页面浏览约束解决冗余、不平衡和曝光集中问题，在美团平台实现ARPU提升28.99%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有担保展示广告方法多基于单广告位假设，无法有效优化多广告位页面浏览中的分配问题，面临广告位级冗余、合同不平衡和曝光集中等挑战。

Method: 将分配问题建模为离线二分图匹配问题，引入合同轮盘机制处理广告位独占性，利用页面浏览约束控制曝光，并设计可扩展的分配优化算法以支持大规模部署。

Result: 在美团广告平台线上A/B测试中，70%流量下平均每用户收入（ARPU）提升28.99%，DID分析表明合同稳定性显著改善，商户ROI和平台收入效率均得到提升。

Conclusion: 所提框架在多广告位担保展示广告分配中显著优于现有方法，具有较强的实际部署适用性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Guaranteed display advertising is crucial for platform monetization, yet existing methods often operate under a single-slot assumption, limiting their ability to optimize allocation across multi-slot page views. In this paper, we propose a novel joint optimization framework for multi-slot GD allocation, addressing key challenges such as slot-level redundancy, contract imbalance, and exposure concentration. Our approach formulates the allocation as an offline bipartite matching problem with a contract roulette mechanism for slot exclusivity and Page View constraints for impression control, and incorporates a scalable allocation optimization algorithm for efficient large-scale deployment. Extensive online tests on the Meituan advertising platform demonstrate that our method significantly improves merchant ROI, platform revenue efficiency, and contract fulfillment robustness. Specifically, online A/B tests show a 28.99% increase in Average Revenue Per User under 70% traffic, and DID analysis further indicates improved contract stability, demonstrating the strong applicability and effectiveness of our framework in real-world advertising deployments.

中文摘要翻译: 担保展示广告对于平台变现至关重要，然而现有方法通常基于单广告位假设，限制了其在多广告位页面浏览中优化分配的能力。本文提出了一种面向多广告位GD分配的新型联合优化框架，解决了广告位级冗余、合同不平衡和曝光集中等关键挑战。我们的方法将分配问题建模为离线二分图匹配问题，引入合同轮盘机制处理广告位独占性，并利用页面浏览约束控制曝光，同时设计了一种可扩展的分配优化算法以支持高效的大规模部署。在美团广告平台上进行的广泛线上测试表明，我们的方法显著提升了商户ROI、平台收入效率和合同履行鲁棒性。具体而言，线上A/B测试显示在70%流量下平均每用户收入（ARPU）提升了28.99%，DID分析进一步表明合同稳定性得到改善，证明了我们的框架在实际广告部署中具有较强的适用性和有效性。

</details>


### [124] [Leveraging Self-Paced Curriculum Learning for Enhanced Modality Balance in Multimodal Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21565)
*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自定步调课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，通过双层次难度评估器（话语级和对话级）缓解多模态情感识别中的模态对齐不均和失衡学习问题，在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上显著提升了性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态情感识别中，不同模态（语言、面部表情、语音语调）之间存在模态未对齐和失衡学习问题，导致多模态信息的有效利用受到限制。

Method: 提出一个基于自定步调课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，包含双层次难度评估器（话语级评估细粒度模态特异性难度，对话级评估情感依赖性和模态一致性）和学习调度器（动态引导训练从易到难）。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上，加权F1提升约+1.2%至+6.6%；在MELD上，增益最高达+10.4%。在不同架构和模态设置下均有一致提升。

Conclusion: SPCL作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，能有效缓解模态失衡、提升模型鲁棒性，并在多模态情感识别任务中展现出良好的有效性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态对话情感识别（MERC）是理解人类交互的关键任务，其中融合语言、面部表情和语音语调的多模态方法已取得了显著进展。然而，模态未对齐和失衡学习仍然是主要挑战，限制了多模态信息的有效利用。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种基于自定步调课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架用于MERC。我们引入了一个双层次难度评估器，同时捕捉话语级和对话级的挑战。话语级评分对细粒度的模态特异性难度进行建模，而对话级评分捕获更广泛的对话结构，包括情感依赖性和模态一致性。基于这些评分，学习调度器动态引导训练从简单实例向困难实例推进。通过将SPCL集成到现有的MERC架构中，我们的方法缓解了模态失衡问题并提升了模型鲁棒性。在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在不同架构和模态设置下均取得了一致改进。在IEMOCAP上，SPCL相较于基线模型将加权F1分数提升了约+1.2%至+6.6%，而在MELD上增益最高达+10.4%。这些结果突显了SPCL作为一种轻量级即插即用模块在多模态情感识别中的有效性和泛化能力。

</details>


### [125] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现，在UCI CKD数据集上表现近乎完美（AUROC 1.00）的五种机器学习模型，在迁移到MIMIC-IV外部数据集后性能急剧下降（AUROC降至0.48-0.58），校准误差飙升，共形预测覆盖率远低于目标值，且所有模型的部署就绪评分均低于4/16，警示仅凭内部测试集的高判别力不足以支撑临床部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 慢性肾脏病（CKD）风险预测的机器学习模型通常只关注内部测试集上的判别性能（如AUROC），而校准质量和不确定性量化很少受到重视，导致临床医生无法判断模型输出的概率是否可靠。本文旨在系统评估模型在校准、不确定性量化和部署就绪性方面的表现，并验证其在外部数据分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Method: 在UCI CKD数据集（400名患者，62.5%患病率）上训练五种分类器：逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、带Platt缩放的SVM和高斯朴素贝叶斯。通过期望校准误差（ECE）、Brier分数评估校准质量，采用分裂共形预测（split conformal prediction）以90%边际覆盖率为目标量化不确定性，并使用八项标准部署就绪框架评估模型。进一步将最优校准变体应用于MIMIC-IV演示队列（97名患者，23.7%患病率），进行分布压力测试，考察患病率偏移和特征缺失下的行为。

Result: 五种模型在UCI测试集上均达到AUROC 1.00的完美判别性能。等渗回归校准将内部ECE降至0.000-0.022。但在MIMIC-IV上，AUROC骤降至0.48-0.58，ECE升至0.68-0.76，共形覆盖率从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25（目标90%）。所有模型的部署就绪检查表得分均未超过4/16。

Conclusion: 近乎完美的内部性能无法迁移到外部数据。在临床预测模型部署之前，必须在外部分布上评估校准稳定性和共形预测覆盖率。

Original Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 用于慢性肾脏病（CKD）风险预测的机器学习模型通常在内部测试集上表现出很强的判别性能，但校准和不确定性量化远未受到足够重视，导致临床医生无法获得关于概率输出是否准确的可靠信息。我们在UCI CKD数据集（400名患者，62.5% CKD患病率）上训练了五种分类器：逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、带Platt缩放的SVM以及高斯朴素贝叶斯。我们从校准质量、共形预测覆盖率和一套八项标准的部署就绪框架三个方面对每个模型进行了评估。通过分布压力测试，将每个模型的最优校准变体应用于开放获取的MIMIC-IV演示队列（97名患者，23.7% CKD），以考察其在患病率偏移和特征缺失下的行为。我们使用期望校准误差（ECE）和Brier分数测量了Platt缩放和等渗回归前后的校准性能，并通过以90%边际覆盖率为目标的分裂共形预测来量化不确定性。五种模型在UCI测试集上均达到AUROC 1.00。等渗回归校准将内部ECE降至0.000-0.022。在MIMIC-IV上，AUROC降至0.48-0.58，ECE升至0.68-0.76，共形覆盖率从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25（目标90%）。没有模型在部署就绪检查表上得分超过4/16。近乎完美的内部性能无法迁移。在临床预测模型部署之前，必须在外部分布上评估校准稳定性和共形预测覆盖率。

</details>


### [126] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTact，一种显式分解抗体CDR设计为接触预测与序列生成两阶段的级联架构，通过距离偏置交叉注意力和接触加权损失，在CHIMERA-Bench上实现结构质量、表位感知和序列恢复的最佳或竞争性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有抗体CDR设计方法将"哪些CDR位置接触抗原"和"这些位置选什么氨基酸"两个子问题混为一谈，通过统一消息传递迫使模型隐式学习接触推理，导致抗原信号在所有位置上被均匀稀释。

Method: 提出ConTact的三级联架构：(1) 学习表面互补指纹；(2) 预测CDR-抗原接触；(3) 将接触门控的抗原特征注入序列生成头。采用距离偏置交叉注意力模块编码几何先验，以及接触加权交叉熵损失将梯度集中在关键结合位置。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，ConTact相比次优基线RMSD降低7%（结构质量最佳），F1分数比GNN基线高10%（表位感知最佳），序列恢复AAR达0.38（具竞争力）。

Conclusion: 显式分解接触预测与氨基酸选择两个子问题能有效提升抗体CDR设计性能，验证了"先接触后动作"架构在结构质量、表位感知和序列恢复方面的优越性。

Original Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 计算抗体CDR设计方法以抗原结构为条件生成结合环，然而现有架构将两个根本不同的子问题混为一谈：识别哪些CDR位置将接触抗原，以及在这些位置选择氨基酸。这种混淆迫使模型通过统一消息传递隐式学习接触推理，使得抗原信号在所有位置上被同等稀释。我们提出ConTact，一种"先接触后动作"的架构，将CDR设计显式分解为三个级联阶段：学习表面互补指纹、预测CDR-抗原接触、以及将接触门控的抗原特征注入序列生成头。距离偏置的交叉注意力模块编码了偏向空间邻近区域的几何先验，而接触加权的交叉熵损失则将梯度信号集中在关键结合位置。在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，ConTact在多个CDR-H3设计基线中实现了最佳的结构质量（相比次优基线RMSD改进7%）、最佳的表位感知能力（相比GNN基线F1分数提升10%），以及具有竞争力的序列恢复率（AAR 0.38）。

</details>


### [127] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现推理蒸馏中教师token的可靠性具有轨迹级结构（而非局部不确定性），并提出基于位置加权的在线自蒸馏方法PW-OPSD，在多个数学推理基准上取得一致提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于熵的在线自蒸馏方法通过教师熵调节token级监督，但推理任务中高熵既可能表示不确定性也可能表示良性解多样性，导致歧义。作者希望识别这一现象并找到更可靠的教师token可靠性预测指标。

Method: 1) 提出分支可行性诊断（branch-viability diagnostic）来识别教师token可靠性；2) 发现面向序列内的位置得分是最强预测器（AUROC=0.83），而局部不确定性得分仅为0.57；3) 据此提出PW-OPSD，在保持OPSD学生轨迹、特权教师和裁剪前向KL目标的基础上，应用递增的位置权重。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上，位置得分的AUROC达0.83，远超局部不确定性得分（0.57）。PW-OPSD在AIME 2024和AIME 2025上Avg@12分别提升+1.0和+1.1点。在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think上也取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏中教师token的可靠性具有轨迹级结构，可以通过位置权重有效利用，无需额外的教师计算开销。

Original Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

中文摘要翻译: 在线自蒸馏（on-policy self-distillation, OPSD）利用特权教师（privileged teacher）在学生自身的轨迹上训练学生，但其标准目标对所有生成的token赋予相同权重，隐含地假设特权教师目标在学生访问的每个前缀处都具有同等可靠性。现有的基于熵的OPD方法通过用教师熵调节token级监督来缓解这种均匀性，但推理中教师的高熵具有模糊的可靠性含义：它既可以反映不可行的不确定性，也可以反映良性的解多样性。为识别这一现象，我们引入了一个分支可行性诊断（branch-viability diagnostic）。具体而言，我们记录来自特权答案教师提示（privileged-answer teacher prompt）的下一个token备选，在学生提示及其在线策略骨干前缀（on-policy spine prefix）之后强制每个备选，并测试所得到的学生模板续写（student-template continuation）是否能恢复正确答案。在Qwen3-4B上，我们发现一个面向序列内的位置得分（oriented within-sequence position score）是教师token可靠性的最强测试预测器，曲线下面积（AUROC）达到0.83；局部不确定性得分最高仅为0.57。受这种轨迹级结构启发，我们提出了位置加权在线自蒸馏（Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation, PW-OPSD），该方法在保持与OPSD相同的学生轨迹、特权教师传递和裁剪前向KL目标的同时，应用递增的位置权重。在我们使用不同随机种子的综合评估中，由诊断导出的PW-OPSD在AIME 2024和AIME 2025的Avg@12上分别提升了+1.0和+1.1分；在两个来自不同家族的大规模模型——DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think——上的泛化评估也展示了一致的聚合Avg@12提升。这些结果表明，推理蒸馏中教师token的可靠性具有轨迹级结构，并且无需额外的教师计算即可加以利用。

</details>


### [128] [$\textit{BlockFormer}$ : Transformer-based inference from interaction maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21617)
*Eloïse Touron,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Julyan Arbel,Nelle Varoquaux,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer架构的数据驱动方法，利用共享结构从交互图谱（如Hi-C）中推断参数，并成功应用于多种物种的着丝粒定位。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从交互图谱（如Hi-C技术得到的全基因组染色体构象捕获图谱）中推断参数是一个通用的逆问题，但真实数据中实体的数量和大小存在变异性，现有方法难以处理这种变异性。

Method: 采用Transformer架构处理实体数量和大小上的变异性，并设计了一个自定义模拟器来生成大量计算成本低廉的合成训练数据。

Result: 该方法在着丝粒定位问题上，能够准确恢复多种基因组大小不同的物种的着丝粒基因组位置。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的方法结合Transformer架构和合成数据生成，能有效处理交互图谱分析中的变异性问题，并在跨物种着丝粒定位任务上表现出色。

Original Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

中文摘要翻译: 从交互图谱中推断，例如通过全基因组染色体构象捕获技术（尤其是Hi-C）进行着丝粒识别，可以形式化为一个通用的逆问题：给定一个总结实体间通过可变数量和大小分块的成对相互作用的图谱，推断一组参数。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种数据驱动的方法，利用这些图谱之间的共享结构（例如局部模式之间的全局对齐），同时处理真实数据中实体数量和大小上的变异性。我们的方法依赖于能够处理这种变异性的Transformer架构，以及一个自定义模拟器，用于生成大量且计算成本低廉的合成训练数据。应用于着丝粒定位问题，该方法能够准确恢复跨多种基因组大小不同的物种的基因组位置。

</details>


### [129] [EntmaxKV: Support-Aware Decoding for Entmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21649)
*Gonçalo Duarte,Miguel Couceiro,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntmaxKV 提出了一种面向 α-entmax 注意力机制的原生稀疏解码框架，通过在加载 KV 缓存前利用查询感知的页面评分和支持感知的候选选择，实现精确的稀疏解码，在 1M 上下文长度下获得最高 5.43 倍加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长上下文解码中，KV 缓存大小随序列长度线性增长，导致内存带宽成为瓶颈。现有的稀疏解码方法基于 softmax 注意力设计，其密集尾部导致任何截断都会丢弃非零概率质量，无法实现精确稀疏解码。

Method: 提出 EntmaxKV 框架：1) 查询感知的页面评分（query-aware page scoring）；2) 支持感知的候选选择（support-aware candidate selection）；3) 稀疏 entmax 注意力。引入高斯感知的 entmax 选择器（Gaussian-aware entmax selector），通过轻量级页面统计估计 entmax 阈值，自适应调整选择的预算。

Result: 在匹配的 KV 预算下，EntmaxKV 比基于 softmax 的稀疏解码方法丢弃更少的概率质量、保留更多支持令牌、获得更低的输出误差。在 1M 上下文长度下，相比全注意力基线，softmax 场景获得 3.36 倍加速，entmax 场景获得 5.43 倍加速。

Conclusion: EntmaxKV 通过原生利用 entmax 的稀疏性质，将稀疏解码问题从近似尾部截断转化为精确的支持恢复，在保持解码精度的同时显著降低 KV 缓存访问成本，为长上下文高效解码提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly limited by KV-cache memory traffic since each generated token attends over a cache whose size grows linearly with context length. Existing sparse decoding methods reduce this cost by selecting subsets of tokens or pages, but are designed for softmax attention, whose dense tails make any truncation discard nonzero probability mass. In contrast, $α$-entmax produces exact zeros, turning sparse decoding from dense-tail approximation into support recovery: if the selected candidates contain the entmax support, sparse decoding remains exact. While recent entmax kernels enable efficient training, they do not address the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, where dense inference still streams the full KV cache before sparsity is known. In this work, we introduce EntmaxKV, an entmax-native sparse decoding framework that exploits sparsity before KV pages are loaded. EntmaxKV combines query-aware page scoring, support-aware candidate selection, and sparse entmax attention. We analyze truncation error through the dropped probability mass $δ$, showing that output error is controlled by $δ$ and vanishes when the entmax support is recovered. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware entmax selector that estimates the entmax threshold from lightweight page statistics, adapting the selected budget to the score distribution. Empirically, EntmaxKV drops less probability mass, retains more support tokens, and achieves lower output error than softmax-based sparse decoding at matched KV budgets. On long-context and language modeling benchmarks, it closely matches full-cache entmax while using a small fraction of the KV cache, achieving up to $3.36\times$ (softmax) and $5.43\times$ (entmax) speedup over full attention baselines at 1M context length. Code available at: https://github.com/deep-spin/entmaxkv.

中文摘要翻译: 长上下文解码正日益受到 KV 缓存内存带宽的限制，因为每个生成的令牌都需要对一个大小随上下文长度线性增长的缓存进行注意力计算。现有的稀疏解码方法通过选择令牌或页面的子集来降低这一开销，但它们是为 softmax 注意力设计的，softmax 的密集尾部使得任何截断都会丢弃非零概率质量。相比之下，α-entmax 能够产生精确的零值，将稀疏解码从密集尾部近似转变为支持恢复问题：如果所选候选集包含 entmax 支持集，则稀疏解码仍然保持精确。尽管最近的 entmax 内核支持高效训练，但它们并未解决自回归解码的瓶颈问题——在稀疏性未知之前，密集推理仍然需要流式传输完整的 KV 缓存。在这项工作中，我们提出了 EntmaxKV，一个 entmax 原生的稀疏解码框架，它在加载 KV 页面之前利用稀疏性。EntmaxKV 结合了查询感知的页面评分、支持感知的候选选择和稀疏 entmax 注意力。我们通过丢弃的概率质量 δ 来分析截断误差，结果表明输出误差受 δ 控制，并且当 entmax 支持集被恢复时误差消失。我们进一步引入了一个高斯感知的 entmax 选择器，它从轻量级的页面统计量中估计 entmax 阈值，根据分数分布自适应地调整所选的预算。实验表明，在匹配的 KV 预算下，EntmaxKV 比基于 softmax 的稀疏解码方法丢弃更少的概率质量、保留更多的支持令牌，并获得更低的输出误差。在长上下文和语言建模基准测试中，EntmaxKV 在使用一小部分 KV 缓存的情况下，紧密匹配全缓存 entmax 的性能，在 1M 上下文长度下，相比全注意力基线实现了最高 3.36 倍（softmax）和 5.43 倍（entmax）的加速。代码已开源：https://github.com/deep-spin/entmaxkv。

</details>


### [130] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现AI文本检测器并非构建AI与人类的分类边界，而是放大了预训练编码器中已存在的“典型性”轴。通过多种实验（投影分析、微调对比、非母语写作反转、冻结探针、雅可比预测器等）验证了这一观点，并证明多数检测性能差异源于校准偏移而非表征学习。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI文本检测方法通常假设检测器是在学习AI与人类文本之间的分类边界，本文旨在挑战这一假设，揭示检测器实际运作机制——它们只是放大了预训练编码器中固有的典型性（typicality）轴。

Method: 1. 在原始编码器（无任务监督）上，通过质心投影（centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3)）进行典型性轴测量；2. 使用冻结探针（24样本）、封闭式雅可比预测器进行轴操作干预；3. 通过文本表面特征残差化、几何符号消融、封闭式文本对预测器三种探针验证观察者不变性；4. 进行匹配TPR评估下的干预动物园校准等价性分析。

Result: 1. 原始编码器质心投影在NYT-vs-HC3上达到AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834（为微调判别上限的86-106%）；2. 非母语ESL写作上AUROC反转至0.06-0.20；3. 24样本冻结探针匹配全微调性能（0.900 vs 0.895）；4. 雅可比预测器实现R²=1.000的通用轴操作，将ELECTRA-CE部署TPR从0.000提升至0.904（FPR=1%）；5. 三种探针在三个架构上cos相似度达0.74/0.81/1.00；6. 97%的LoRA→全微调偏差差距来自校准偏移。

Conclusion: AI文本检测器并非学习AI-vs-人类的判别边界，而是放大预训练编码器中已有的典型性轴。该轴在非母语写作上会反转，且绝大多数检测性能差异（如LoRA与全微调之间的差距）源自校准偏移而非真正的表征学习，核心预测得到实验证实。

Original Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

中文摘要翻译: AI文本检测器放大的是预训练典型性轴，而非构建AI与人类的分类边界。在没有任何任务监督的原始编码器上，将投影方向设为质心(AI)-质心(HC3)，在三个架构上分别达到NYT-vs-HC3的AUROC为0.806/0.944/0.834（达到微调判别上限的86-106%：在RoBERTa-base上，原始投影甚至超过微调）；在RoBERTa-base上，全微调在两个流畅正式语料上的判别能力反而低于原始投影。同样的轴在非母语ESL写作上发生反转（AUROC 0.06-0.20）——这是典型性解读独有的可证伪预测。一个24样本的冻结探针匹配了全微调性能（0.900 vs 0.895）。封闭式雅可比预测器以R²=1.000的通用性参数化了轴操作干预，将ELECTRA-CE部署TPR从0.000提升至0.904（FPR=1%），并转移到三个独立训练的第三方RoBERTa检测器上，达到16/16的预言机等价性（OpenAI检测器上NYT-FPR降低57%）。适用范围：编码器家族；机制量级以HC3为锚点；存在跨架构共享的总体典型性轴，但每个文本的具体机制在不同架构间存在差异。三种操作上不同的探针——文本表面大写率残差化、几何符号消融、封闭式文本对预测器——在三个架构上达成cos 0.74/0.81/1.00的一致性，证实了观察者不变性。在匹配TPR=0.90的评估下，已发表的干预动物园（CC, dealign-f2c）在27个单元上校准等价（|ΔAUROC| ≤ 0.0081），且ELECTRA上LoRA到全微调偏差差距中≥97%来自校准偏移而非表征学习——核心声明的预测得到证实。

</details>


### [131] [Value-Gradient Hypothesis of RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21654)
*Arip Asadulaev,Daniil Ognev,Karim Salta,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从值梯度视角解释了无评论家强化学习方法（如PPO和GRPO）在LLM后训练中的有效性，证明了演员更新在期望上等价于值梯度，并通过注意力机制的自动微分近似实现，最终提出了RL有效性的分解准则。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管PPO和GRPO等无评论家强化学习方法在LLM后训练中表现优异，但其有效性的理论原因尚不清晰，需要揭示其工作机制以及适用的条件。

Method: 提出值梯度视角：1）在可微分轨迹和加性噪声参数化下，证明演员更新在期望上等价于值梯度；2）对于离散Transformer策略，证明通过注意力机制的自动微分产生的经验协态近似于值信号，其误差由采样间隔和策略熵控制。

Result: 1）无评论家RL的演员更新在期望上具有值梯度形式；2）Transformer的自动微分能近似值梯度信号，误差可控；3）RL的影响可分解为值梯度信号和可达奖励裕度，从而给出RL在预训练轨迹中最有效的判据。

Conclusion: 从理论上阐明了无评论家RL方法在LLM后训练中有效的原因，并提出了预测RL最大收益的条件准则。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. We develop a value-gradient perspective of critic-free RL for LLM post-training. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习显著改进了预训练语言模型，但无评论家方法（如PPO和GRPO）为何有效以及何时能带来最大收益仍缺乏深入研究。我们提出了一种从值梯度视角理解LLM后训练中无评论家RL的方法。首先，在可微分轨迹和加性噪声参数化下，我们证明演员更新在期望上具有值梯度的形式：反向传播传递的协态变量的条件期望等于值梯度。其次，对于离散Transformer策略，我们证明通过注意力机制的自动微分产生的经验协态近似于该值信号，其误差由采样间隔和策略熵控制。这些结果促使我们将RL的影响分解为值梯度信号和可达奖励裕度，从而给出了沿预训练轨迹RL最有效的判据。

</details>


### [132] [Hierarchical Variational Policies for Reward-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21661)
*Kushagra Pandey,Farrin Marouf Sofian,Jan Niklas Groeneveld,Felix Draxler,Stephan Mandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层变分模型框架，将扩散模型对下游任务的测试时适应通过轻量级随机策略进行摊销，实现了高质量奖励对齐样本的快速生成，在超分辨率等逆问题上比现有方法推理速度提升5倍以上且质量更优。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 预训练扩散模型在适配下游目标（如逆问题）时，通常需要昂贵的测试时引导或优化，推理成本过高，缺乏高效的样本生成方法。

Method: 将测试时适应形式化为分层变分模型，通过轻量级但表达能力强的随机策略摊销控制，支持大步长的少步扩散采样，实现快速推理同时保持样本质量；进一步扩展为半摊销机制，结合廉价摊销提议与有限测试时优化。

Result: 在4倍超分辨率任务上，该方法比最优基线实现了更好感知质量的同时推理速度提升5倍以上；在半摊销机制下，在多个具有挑战性的逆问题上达到最先进的感知质量。

Conclusion: 提出的全摊销采样器实现了优异的质量-速度权衡，半摊销扩展进一步达到逆问题上的最先进感知质量，显著降低了推理计算成本。

Original Abstract: Adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream objectives such as inverse problems often requires expensive test-time guidance or optimization. We propose a principled framework for generating high-quality reward-aligned samples at substantially reduced inference cost. Our approach formulates test-time adaptation as a hierarchical variational model, where control is amortized into a lightweight yet expressive stochastic policy. This formulation naturally supports few-step diffusion sampling: large step sizes enable fast inference, while the learned policy maintains sample quality by providing structured per-step control. The resulting fully amortized sampler achieves a strong quality--speed tradeoff, matching or exceeding recent test-time scaling baselines while requiring significantly less compute. For example, on 4x super-resolution, our method achieves better perceptual quality with more than 5x faster inference compared to the best-performing baseline. We further extend our approach to a semi-amortized regime that combines cheap amortized proposals with limited test-time optimization, achieving state-of-the-art perceptual quality across several challenging inverse problems.

中文摘要翻译: 将预训练扩散模型适配到下游目标（如逆问题）通常需要昂贵的测试时引导或优化。我们提出了一种原则性框架，能够在显著降低推理成本的同时生成高质量奖励对齐样本。我们的方法将测试时适应形式化为分层变分模型，其中控制通过轻量级但表达能力强的随机策略进行摊销。该公式自然支持少步扩散采样：大步长实现快速推理，而学习到的策略通过提供结构化的每步控制来保持样本质量。由此产生的全摊销采样器实现了优异的质量-速度权衡，在匹配或超越近期测试时扩展基线的同时，所需计算量显著更少。例如，在4倍超分辨率任务上，与表现最佳的基线相比，我们的方法实现了更好的感知质量且推理速度提升超过5倍。我们进一步将方法扩展到半摊销机制，该机制将廉价的摊销提议与有限的测试时优化相结合，在多个具有挑战性的逆问题上达到了最先进的感知质量。

</details>


### [133] [I-SAFE: Wasserstein Coherence Metrics for Structural Auditing of Scientific AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21731)
*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出I-SAFE框架，一种基于Wasserstein相干性度量（WCM）的事后分布审计方法，用于评估科学AI模型在结构化扰动下的输出分布相干性，弥补传统仅依赖准确率评估的不足。在药物-靶标相互作用预测任务中，I-SAFE揭示了三类模型虽预测性能相近但分布响应特征显著不同。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度学习模型在科学预测任务中常因利用捷径特征或数据集偏差而表现良好，但实际并未学到与领域相关的高质量结构。仅依赖准确率等基准指标无法检测此类问题，亟需一种能够评估模型是否真正对齐领域知识的审计方法。

Method: 提出I-SAFE框架，核心是Wasserstein相干性度量（WCM）。给定训练好的黑盒预测器和外部结构先验（编码领域知识），对输入施加结构引导的扰动，通过三个互补指标评估输出分布相干性：基于分位数的度量（QBM）评估位置级相干性，WCM评估序数相干性，平移不变WCM评估形状相干性。

Result: 在Davis激酶基准、KLIFS结合口袋注释上对DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA和TAPB三个序列型DTI模型进行审计。结果表明，尽管模型预测性能相当，I-SAFE揭示了它们截然不同的分布响应特征，而这种差异无法通过基于准确率的评估发现。

Conclusion: I-SAFE框架是模型无关的，适用于任何输入可分解且存在外部先验的领域，为科学AI模型的可靠性评估提供了超越准确率的新视角。

Original Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习模型越来越多地用于科学预测任务，在这些任务中，强大的基准性能通常被解释为具有科学意义的行为的证据。这种解释是脆弱的，因为模型可能利用捷径特征、数据集特定的规律性或分布偏差，这些在保留数据上具有预测性，但与领域相关的结构并不对齐。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了I-SAFE（Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable）框架，这是一种以Wasserstein相干性度量（WCM）为中心的科学AI模型事后分布审计框架。给定一个训练好的黑盒预测器和一个编码任务相关输入结构领域知识的外部结构先验，I-SAFE在结构引导的输入扰动下评估原始模型输出。所提出的审计通过三个互补的度量来测量输出分布相干性：用于位置级相干性的基于分位数的度量（QBM）、用于序数相干性的WCM，以及用于形状相干性的平移不变WCM变体。我们在药物-靶标相互作用（DTI）预测任务上实例化了I-SAFE，使用Davis激酶基准、KLIFS（激酶-配体相互作用指纹与结构）结合口袋注释，以及三个基于序列的DTI模型：DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA和TAPB。尽管这些模型在可比的预测范围内运行，但I-SAFE揭示了截然不同的分布响应特征，这是基于准确率的评估无法看到的区别。该框架是模型无关的，适用于任何输入可分解且存在外部先验的领域。

</details>


### [134] [Quantitative coronary calcification analysis for prediction of myocardial ischemia using non-contrast CT calcium scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21745)
*Juhwan Lee,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Hao Wu,Justin N. Kim,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于机器学习的新框架，利用非增强CT钙化扫描（CTCS）中的定量冠状动脉钙化特征（钙组学）来预测心肌缺血。该模型结合了Agatston评分、8个钙组学特征和年龄，在1,375名患者的数据上达到了98.9%的精度和79.2%的灵敏度，证明钙组学特征能提供超越传统风险因素和Agatston评分的增量预测价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 非增强CT钙化扫描（CTCS）已被广泛用于心血管风险分层，但传统上仅使用Agatston评分等有限指标。本研究旨在开发一种新颖的机器学习框架，利用常规非增强CTCS扫描中的定量冠状动脉钙化评估来预测心肌缺血，以提升风险分层的可及性和准确性。

Method: 研究分析了1,375名同时接受非增强CTCS和regadenoson负荷心脏PET心肌灌注成像的患者数据，提取了74个变量（包括临床变量、Agatston评分和钙组学特征）。使用XGBoost结合SHAP（Shapley Additive Explanations）进行特征选择，采用5折交叉验证训练和评估预测模型。

Result: 在987名患者中，89名（9%）心肌缺血阳性。最终模型包含Agatston评分、8个钙组学特征和年龄，精度达98.9±3.0%，灵敏度79.2±8.4%，F1分数87.7±5.3%。加入钙组学特征后，模型预测性能显著优于仅使用临床变量或临床变量加Agatston评分的模型（p<0.05）。值得注意的是，钙化动脉数量虽在SHAP分析中排名最低，但在逻辑回归分析中与心肌缺血关联最强（OR: 3.63, 95%CI: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001）。

Conclusion: 本研究开发了一种利用常规非增强CTCS扫描预测心肌缺血的机器学习方法。钙组学特征能提供超越传统风险因素和Agatston评分的增量预测价值，可能有助于实现更可及的心血管风险分层。

Original Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is widely recognized as an effective tool for cardiovascular risk stratification. This study aimed to develop a novel machine learning framework for predicting myocardial ischemia from routine non-contrast CTCS scans using quantitative coronary calcium assessment. This study analyzed 1,375 patients who underwent both non-contrast CTCS and regadenoson stress cardiac positron emission tomography myocardial perfusion imaging within one year at University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center. A total of 74 variables, including clinical variables, Agatston score, and calcium-omics features, were evaluated. Relevant features were identified using XGBoost with Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Predictive models were trained and evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation. Among 987 patients, 89 (9%) were positive for myocardial ischemia. The final model incorporated the Agatston score, eight calcium-omics features, and age. The proposed model achieved a precision of 98.9+/-3.0%, sensitivity of 79.2+/-8.4, and F1 score of 87.7+/-5.3%. The addition of calcium-omics features significantly improved predictive performance compared with models using clinical variables alone or clinical variables with the Agatston score (p<0.05). Interestingly, the number of calcified arteries, despite being the lowest-ranked feature based on SHAP analysis, showed the strongest association with myocardial ischemia in logistic regression analysis (odds ratio: 3.63, 95% confidence interval: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001). We developed a machine learning approach for predicting myocardial ischemia using routinely acquired non-contrast CTCS scans. Calcium-omics features provided incremental predictive value beyond conventional risk factors and Agatston scoring and may support more accessible cardiovascular risk stratification.

中文摘要翻译: 非对比计算机断层扫描钙化评分（CTCS）被广泛认为是心血管风险分层的有效工具。本研究旨在开发一种新颖的机器学习框架，利用常规非增强CTCS扫描中的定量冠状动脉钙化评估来预测心肌缺血。本研究分析了在University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center一年内同时接受非增强CTCS和regadenoson负荷心脏正电子发射断层扫描心肌灌注成像的1,375名患者。共评估了74个变量，包括临床变量、Agatston评分和钙组学特征。使用XGBoost结合Shapley Additive Explanations（SHAP）识别相关特征。采用5折交叉验证训练和评估预测模型。在987名患者中，89名（9%）心肌缺血阳性。最终模型包含Agatston评分、8个钙组学特征和年龄。该模型实现了98.9±3.0%的精度、79.2±8.4%的灵敏度和87.7±5.3%的F1分数。与仅使用临床变量或临床变量加Agatston评分的模型相比，加入钙组学特征显著提升了预测性能（p<0.05）。有趣的是，钙化动脉数量尽管在SHAP分析中排名最低，但在逻辑回归分析中与心肌缺血的关联最强（优势比：3.63，95%置信区间：2.80-4.77，p<0.00001）。我们开发了一种利用常规采集的非增强CTCS扫描预测心肌缺血的机器学习方法。钙组学特征提供了超越传统风险因素和Agatston评分的增量预测价值，可能有助于实现更可及的心血管风险分层。

</details>


### [135] [Models Can Model, But Can't Bind: Structured Grounding in Text-to-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21751)
*Zhiqi Gao,Albert Ge,Alexander Berenbeim,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Text2Opt-Bench基准测试，发现文本到优化任务中存在“有效绑定极限”问题，并设计了BIND方法——通过将数值数据外化到结构化文件，让模型以编程方式绑定数据而非从提示中转录，显著提升了GPT-5-Nano（59.1%→82.4%）和GPT-5（86.2%→95.8%）的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 文本到优化需要两个可分离的能力：建模（选择正确的优化结构）和绑定（将每个系数、索引和参数与实际问题的具体数据对应起来）。现有模型在实例数据增长时准确率显著下降，即使问题本身形式简单，这被称为“有效绑定极限”。

Method: 提出BIND方法，一种简单的推理时方法，将数值数据外化到结构化文件中，使模型通过编程方式绑定数据而非从提示中逐字转录。此外，通过在绑定任务上专精微调模型，验证了建模与绑定可分离的假设。

Result: BIND将GPT-5-Nano从59.1%提升至82.4%，匹配pass@5（82.0%）但token成本低于pass@1；GPT-5从86.2%提升至95.8%。仅针对绑定任务微调的1.5B参数量专精模型，效果匹配7B端到端基线模型。

Conclusion: 文本到优化中“建模”与“绑定”两种能力是可分离的，通过将数据绑定外化处理可以有效突破有效绑定极限，提升模型在处理大规模优化问题时的准确性，且绑定专精模型可以较小的参数量达到甚至超越更大的端到端模型。

Original Abstract: Text-to-optimization requires two separable capabilities: modeling -- choosing the right optimization structure -- and binding -- grounding every coefficient, index, and parameter in the concrete problem data. We study this via Text2Opt-Bench, a scalable benchmark of solver-verified optimization problems spanning 12 categories, from textbook linear programs to stochastic and multi-objective formulations with up to thousands of variables. Across 10+ models, we find that accuracy collapses as instance data grows, even when the formulation itself is simple. We call this the effective binding limit. We address this via a simple inference-time approach, BIND, which externalizes numeric data to structured files so the model binds data programmatically rather than transcribing from the prompt. BIND improves GPT-5-Nano from 59.1% to 82.4% accuracy, matching pass@5 (82.0%) at lower token cost than pass@1, and GPT-5 from 86.2% to 95.8%. Furthermore, we validate our hypothesis by finetuning a model exclusively on binding and show that it outperforms end-to-end SFT and RL across three structurally distinct optimization categories, with a 1.5B binding specialist alone matching a 7B end-to-end baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到优化需要两个可分离的能力：建模——选择正确的优化结构，以及绑定——将每个系数、索引和参数与实际问题的具体数据对应起来。我们通过Text2Opt-Bench来研究这一问题，这是一个可扩展的、经过求解器验证的优化问题基准测试，涵盖12个类别，从教科书中的线性规划到具有数千变量的随机和多目标优化。在10多个模型上，我们发现当实例数据增长时，准确率会急剧下降，即使问题形式本身很简单。我们将此称为有效绑定极限。我们通过一种简单的推理时方法BIND来解决这个问题，该方法将数值数据外化到结构化文件中，使模型以编程方式绑定数据，而不是从提示中转录数据。BIND将GPT-5-Nano的准确率从59.1%提升至82.4%，与pass@5（82.0%）相当，但token成本低于pass@1；同时将GPT-5的准确率从86.2%提升至95.8%。此外，我们通过在绑定任务上专精微调模型来验证我们的假设，结果表明，该模型在三个结构不同的优化类别上均优于端到端SFT和RL方法，一个1.5B的绑定专精模型单独就能匹配7B的端到端基线模型。

</details>


### [136] [PEARL: Unbiased Percentile Estimation via Contrastive Learning for Industrial-Scale Livestream Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21752)
*Blake Gella,Wei Wu,Yuhao Yin,Zexi Huang,Zikai Wang,Emily Liu,Junlin Zhang,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出非参数对比百分位框架PEARL，通过相对偏好信号建模缓解用户行为强度不平衡偏差，在离线实验和数十亿用户规模的直播平台在线A/B测试中显著提升推荐性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 推荐系统受用户行为强度不平衡影响，活跃用户信号被过度放大而其他用户被低估，导致推荐质量下降。现有方法依赖辅助分布估计模型，存在额外偏差风险。

Method: 提出PEARL框架：(1) 非参数对比百分位近似，利用真实对比交互样本直接估计百分位关系；(2) 相对优势去偏，提供成对比较的无偏估计理论保证；(3) 预测引导的bootstrap机制处理稀疏离散反馈；(4) 广义值加权公式和协同训练策略增强灵活性与表示学习。

Result: 离线实验证明PEARL有效缓解行为偏差并提升多排序目标性能。在线A/B测试（数十亿用户直播平台）：观看时长+2.10%，消费金额+0.80%，互动率+1.49%，举报率-6.91%。

Conclusion: PEARL通过相对偏好信号建模有效解决行为强度不平衡问题，无需辅助分布估计模型，在理论和实践上均验证了有效性，适用于大规模推荐系统。

Original Abstract: Recommender systems trained on user interaction data are susceptible to behavioral intensity imbalance--a systematic distortion arising from heterogeneous engagement patterns across users. This imbalance skews feedback signals such that observed interactions no longer faithfully reflect true preferences, causing models to disproportionately amplify signals from highly active users while underrepresenting others, which ultimately degrades recommendation quality and robustness at scale. To address this issue, we propose a nonparametric contrastive percentile approximation framework, PEARL, that models relative preference signals instead of absolute engagement magnitudes. Building upon relative advantage debiasing, PEARL leverages real contrastive interaction samples to approximate percentile relationships directly, without relying on auxiliary distribution estimation models. We provide theoretical justification demonstrating that such pairwise comparisons yield unbiased estimates of percentile-based preference signals. For broader applicability, we introduce a prediction-based bootstrapping mechanism for percentile smoothing to handle sparse and discrete feedback, alongside a generalized value-weighted formulation and a co-training strategy to enhance both modeling flexibility and representation learning. Extensive offline experiments demonstrate that PEARL effectively mitigates behavioral bias and consistently improves recommendation performance across multiple ranking targets. Deployed in a production livestream platform with a combined user base of billions, online A/B testing confirms substantial real-world gains: +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate.

中文摘要翻译: 基于用户交互数据训练的推荐系统容易受到行为强度不平衡的影响——这是一种由用户间异质性参与模式引起的系统性失真。这种不平衡扭曲了反馈信号，使得观测到的交互不再真实反映用户的真实偏好，导致模型不成比例地放大高活跃用户的信号而低估其他用户，最终损害推荐质量和大规模鲁棒性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种非参数对比百分位近似框架PEARL，该框架对相对偏好信号而非绝对参与强度进行建模。基于相对优势去偏，PEARL利用真实对比交互样本直接近似百分位关系，无需依赖辅助分布估计模型。我们提供理论证明表明，这种成对比较能够产生基于百分位的偏好信号的无偏估计。为提升普适性，我们引入了一种基于预测的自举机制用于百分位平滑以处理稀疏和离散反馈，同时提出广义值加权公式和协同训练策略以增强建模灵活性和表示学习。大量离线实验表明，PEARL有效缓解了行为偏差，并在多个排序目标上持续提升推荐性能。在拥有数十亿用户规模的直播平台生产环境中部署后，在线A/B测试证实了显著的现实收益：观看时长+2.10%，消费金额+0.80%，互动率+1.49%，举报率-6.91%。

</details>


### [137] [Machine learning prediction of obstructive coronary artery disease using opportunistic coronary calcium and epicardial fat assessments from CT calcium scoring scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21762)
*Juhwan Lee,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Justin N. Kim,Mohamed H. E. Makhlouf,Michelle C. Williams,David E. Newby,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用非对比CT钙化评分（CTCS）图像，提取钙化组学和心外膜脂肪组学特征，结合CatBoost-SHAP方法进行特征选择与建模，成功预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病（CAD），模型在包括零钙化积分患者中均表现出高准确性和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CTCS是一种低成本、广泛使用的成像方式，但传统方法主要依赖钙化积分单一指标，对阻塞性CAD的预测能力有限。本研究旨在通过机器学习框架全面挖掘CTCS图像中钙化和心外膜脂肪的定量特征，提升预测性能，减少对增强或侵入性检查的依赖。

Method: 研究纳入SCOT-HEART临床试验中1,324名患者，从CTCS图像中提取24项临床变量、189个钙化组学特征和211个心外膜脂肪组学特征。采用CatBoost算法结合SHAP值进行特征选择，最终筛选出14个最具预测力的特征，并使用CatBoost梯度提升方法构建预测模型。

Result: 模型在424个候选特征中筛选出14个最优特征，其中前2个来自脂肪组学，其余12个来自钙化组学。模型性能：灵敏度83.1±4.6%，特异性93.8±1.7%，准确率85.3±2.0%，F1分数73.9±3.3%。钙化组学和脂肪组学的引入显著提升了预测性能，且模型在零钙化积分的阻塞性CAD患者中仍保持可靠的预测能力。

Conclusion: 该创新方法有望改善临床决策，特别是在低至中风险患者群体中，可减少对对比增强或侵入性诊断程序的依赖。

Original Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is a cost-effective imaging modality widely used to detect coronary artery calcifications. This study aimed to develop an advanced machine learning framework that utilizes quantitative analyses of coronary calcium and epicardial fat from CTCS images to predict obstructive coronary artery disease (CAD). The study population consisted of 1,324 patients from the SCOT-HEART clinical trial who underwent both CTCS and coronary CT angiography. We extracted and analyzed a broad range of features, including 24 clinical variables, 189 calcium-omics, and 211 epicardial fat-omics features from the CTCS images. Feature selection was conducted using the CatBoost algorithm combined with SHapley Additive exPlanation (SHAP) values. Predictive modeling utilized the CatBoost gradient boosting method, focusing on the most informative features. From an initial set of 424 candidate features, 14 were identified as most predictive through the CatBoost-SHAP method. The top two predictive features originated from fat-omics, with the remaining 12 features derived from calcium-omics. The optimized model achieved robust predictive capabilities, demonstrating a sensitivity of 83.1+/-4.6%, specificity of 93.8+/-1.7%, accuracy of 85.3+/-2.0%, and an F1 score of 73.9+/-3.3%. Inclusion of calcium-omics and fat-omics data significantly improved predictive performance. Notably, the model also showed reliable predictive accuracy in patients with diverse coronary calcium scores, including cases with obstructive CAD despite a zero-calcium score. This innovative approach holds promise for improving clinical decision-making and potentially reducing dependence on contrast-enhanced or invasive diagnostic procedures, particularly within low-to intermediate-risk patient groups.

中文摘要翻译: 非对比CT钙化评分（CTCS）是一种经济高效的成像方式，广泛用于检测冠状动脉钙化。本研究旨在开发一种先进的机器学习框架，利用CTCS图像中冠状动脉钙化和心外膜脂肪的定量分析来预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病（CAD）。研究人群包括来自SCOT-HEART临床试验的1,324名患者，这些患者同时接受了CTCS和冠状动脉CT血管造影。我们从CTCS图像中提取并分析了广泛的特征，包括24项临床变量、189个钙化组学特征和211个心外膜脂肪组学特征。采用CatBoost算法结合SHapley Additive exPlanation（SHAP）值进行特征选择。预测建模使用了CatBoost梯度提升方法，聚焦于最具信息量的特征。从初始的424个候选特征中，通过CatBoost-SHAP方法筛选出14个最具预测力的特征。前两个最具预测力的特征来自脂肪组学，其余12个特征来自钙化组学。优化后的模型展现出强大的预测能力，灵敏度为83.1±4.6%，特异性为93.8±1.7%，准确率为85.3±2.0%，F1分数为73.9±3.3%。钙化组学和脂肪组学数据的引入显著提升了预测性能。值得注意的是，该模型在不同钙化积分的患者中均表现出可靠的预测准确性，包括零钙化积分但存在阻塞性CAD的病例。这一创新方法有望改善临床决策，并可能减少对对比增强或侵入性诊断程序的依赖，尤其是在低至中风险患者群体中。

</details>


### [138] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限折扣MDP中基于递归OCE风险测度的风险敏感强化学习，通过模型驱动方法给出了值学习与策略学习的PAC可学习性完整刻画，并证明了当效用函数定义域非全实数时问题不可PAC学习，同时给出了紧的下界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的风险敏感强化学习研究多关注特定风险测度（如CVaR、熵风险等），缺乏对一类通用风险测度（OCE）的系统性样本复杂度分析，尤其是在递归设定下值函数和策略的可学习性与不可学习性边界尚不明确。

Method: 采用基于生成模型的模型驱动方法，对递归OCE下的最优状态-动作值函数和最优策略进行PAC样本复杂度分析。通过精确刻画效用函数u的性质与PAC可学习性之间的关系，并建立上下界。

Result: (1) 给出了效用函数u使得对应OCE目标PAC可学习的精确条件；(2) 证明了当dom(u)≠ℝ时问题不可PAC学习；(3) 值学习和策略学习的下界在状态-动作空间大小SA上是紧的；(4) 对CVaR_τ，正确依赖为1/τ²，相比现有最优结果提升1/τ因子，但在1/(1-γ)依赖上非最优。

Conclusion: 递归OCE下风险敏感强化学习的可学习性完全由效用函数的定义域决定；模型驱动方法可以得到紧的样本复杂度边界；CVaR的τ依赖获得了理论改进，但有效折扣长度上的依赖尚待进一步优化。

Original Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了有限折扣MDP中的风险敏感强化学习问题，假设MDP的生成模型可用。我们考虑一类称为优化确定性等价（OCE）的风险测度族，其包含重要的风险测度如熵风险、CVaR和均值-方差。我们聚焦于在递归OCE下学习最优状态-动作值函数（值学习）和最优策略（策略学习）的样本复杂度。我们给出了效用函数$u$使得对应OCE定义PAC可学习目标的精确刻画。我们分析了一种简单的模型驱动方法，并推导了PAC样本复杂度边界。我们证明，当$u$的定义域不是全实数集$\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$时，对应问题不可PAC学习。最后，我们为值学习和策略学习建立了相应的下界，证明其在状态-动作空间大小$SA$上的紧性，并且对于更受限的一类效用函数，我们推导了显式依赖于有效折扣长度$\frac{1}{1-γ}$的下界。具体地，对于$\text{CVaR}_τ$，我们展示了其对$τ$的正确依赖为$\frac{1}{τ^2}$，从而相比现有最优结果改进了一个$\frac{1}{τ}$因子，尽管我们的边界在$\frac{1}{1-γ}$上的依赖是非最优的。

</details>


### [139] [Memory-R2: Fair Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21768)
*Sikuan Yan,Ahmed Bahloul,Ercong Nie,Susanna Schwarzmann,Riccardo Trivisonno,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Memory-R2 是一个针对长 horizon 记忆增强型 LLM 智能体的训练框架，其核心算法 LoGo-GRPO 通过结合局部和全局组相对优化来解决因记忆状态不一致导致的信用分配问题，并采用共享参数联合设计与渐进式课程训练策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在多会话环境下使用强化学习训练记忆增强型 LLM 智能体时，不同 rollout 会产生不同的中间记忆状态，使得轨迹级比较不公平，违反 GRPO 等组相对方法的关键假设，导致轨迹级奖励为长 horizon 记忆操作提供噪声或偏置的信用信号。

Method: 提出 LoGo-GRPO 算法，结合局部和全局组相对优化：全局目标保留从长 horizon 轨迹级奖励中端到端学习，局部重 rollout 从相同中间记忆状态比较不同记忆操作结果；采用共享参数联合设计，事实提取器和记忆管理器由同一 LLM 主干通过角色提示实例化；采用渐进式课程训练，将训练 horizon 从 8 逐步增至 16 再到 32 会话。

Result: 论文为方法介绍类工作，未明确报告具体实验结果，但提出了完整的训练框架和算法设计。

Conclusion: Memory-R2 提供了一种有效的训练范式，用于在长 horizon 多会话场景下训练记忆增强型 LLM 智能体，通过解决记忆状态不一致问题实现更公平的组比较和更精确的记忆构建监督。

Original Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents enable interactions that extend beyond finite context windows by storing, updating, and reusing information across sessions. However, training such agents with reinforcement learning in multi-session environments is challenging because memory turns the agent's past actions into part of its future environment. Once different rollouts write, update, or delete different memories, they no longer share the same intermediate memory state, making trajectory-level comparisons fundamentally unfair. This violates a key assumption behind group-relative methods such as GRPO, where rollouts are compared as if they were sampled from the same effective environment. Consequently, trajectory-level rewards provide noisy or biased credit signals for long-horizon memory operations. To address this challenge, we introduce Memory-R2, a training framework for long-horizon memory-augmented LLM agents. Its core algorithm, LoGo-GRPO, combines local and global group-relative optimization. The global objective preserves end-to-end learning from long-horizon trajectory-level rewards, while local rerollouts compare different memory-operation outcomes from the same intermediate memory state, yielding fairer group comparisons and more precise supervision for memory construction. Beyond credit assignment, Memory-R2 jointly optimizes memory formation and memory evolution with a shared-parameter co-learning design, where a fact extractor and a memory manager are instantiated from the same LLM backbone through role-specific prompts. To stabilize multi-step RL over long memory horizons, we adopt a progressive curriculum that increases the training horizon from 8 to 16 to 32 sessions. Together, these components provide an effective training paradigm for memory-augmented LLM agents in long-horizon multi-session settings.

中文摘要翻译: 记忆增强型大语言模型（LLM）智能体能够通过跨会话存储、更新和重用信息，实现超越有限上下文窗口的交互。然而，在多会话环境中使用强化学习训练此类智能体极具挑战性，因为记忆将智能体过去的行动转化为其未来环境的一部分。一旦不同的 rollout 写入、更新或删除不同的记忆，它们便不再共享相同的中间记忆状态，这使得轨迹级比较从根本上失去了公平性。这违反了 GRPO 等组相对方法的关键假设——即 rollout 被视作从同一有效环境中采样进行比较。因此，轨迹级奖励为长 horizon 记忆操作提供了带有噪声或偏置的信用信号。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了 Memory-R2，一个针对长 horizon 记忆增强型 LLM 智能体的训练框架。其核心算法 LoGo-GRPO 结合了局部和全局组相对优化。全局目标保留了从长 horizon 轨迹级奖励中端到端学习的能力，而局部重 rollout 则从相同的中间记忆状态出发比较不同的记忆操作结果，从而产生更公平的组比较并为记忆构建提供更精确的监督。在信用分配之外，Memory-R2 通过共享参数联合设计协同优化记忆形成与记忆演化，其中事实提取器和记忆管理器通过角色提示从同一个 LLM 主干实例化而来。为稳定长记忆 horizon 上的多步强化学习，我们采用了渐进式课程，将训练 horizon 从 8 逐步提升至 16 再到 32 个会话。综上所述，这些组件为在长 horizon 多会话场景下训练记忆增强型 LLM 智能体提供了一种有效的训练范式。

</details>


### [140] [Provable Robustness against Backdoor Attacks via the Primal-Dual Perspective on Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21780)
*Aman Saxena,Jan Schuchardt,Yan Scholten,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私分布（privacy profiles）相结合的统一框架，用于对包含后门攻击在内的复合攻击（训练时+推理时）提供端到端的鲁棒性认证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的随机平滑认证方法通常只针对训练时投毒攻击或推理时逃逸攻击单独设计，难以扩展到后门攻击场景（训练数据和测试数据被联合扰动），因为需要将训练时和推理时的随机化机制纳入同一个鲁棒性证书中进行分析。

Method: 将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私分布概念联系起来，通过隐私分布这一数值化工具对不同机制的组合进行紧致分析，从而构建一个模块化、可组合的端到端认证框架。该框架可复用已有的差分隐私机制分析方法。

Result: 在DP-SGD和深度分区聚合（Deep Partition Aggregation）结合推理时平滑的实例上，推导出了针对训练时和推理时攻击的联合鲁棒性保证，并在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 提供了一个通用且 principled 的框架，能够利用组合机制在更贴近真实攻击能力的复杂威胁模型下认证鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Randomized smoothing is a powerful tool for certifying robustness to adversarial perturbations, including poisoning attacks via randomized training and evasion attacks via randomized inference. Extending these guarantees to backdoor attacks, where training and test data are jointly perturbed, remains challenging because training- and test-time randomized mechanisms must be analyzed within a single robustness certificate. We address this by connecting randomized smoothing to the dual view of differential privacy through privacy profiles, which provide a numerical procedure for composing heterogeneous mechanisms. The resulting framework enables tight, modular, end-to-end certification of complex, composed mechanisms while leveraging existing analyses of differentially private mechanisms. We instantiate the framework for DP-SGD and Deep Partition Aggregation with inference-time smoothing, deriving joint robustness guarantees against both training-time and inference-time attacks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. Overall, we provide a principled and general framework for using composite mechanisms to certify robustness under complex threat models that better capture the capabilities of real-world adversaries.

中文摘要翻译: 随机平滑是一种强大的工具，可用于认证对抗性扰动的鲁棒性，包括通过随机化训练来抵御投毒攻击，以及通过随机化推理来抵御逃逸攻击。然而，将这些保证扩展到后门攻击（即训练数据和测试数据被联合扰动）场景仍然具有挑战性，因为训练时和推理时的随机化机制必须在同一个鲁棒性证书内进行分析。我们通过将随机平滑与差分隐私的隐私分布（privacy profiles）这一对偶视角联系起来，解决了这一问题。隐私分布为组合异构机制提供了一种数值化计算过程。由此产生的框架能够对复杂的组合机制进行紧致、模块化且端到端的认证，同时可复用已有的差分隐私机制分析方法。我们将该框架实例化到DP-SGD和结合推理时平滑的深度分区聚合（Deep Partition Aggregation）上，推导出了针对训练时和推理时攻击的联合鲁棒性保证。在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验验证了该框架的有效性。总体而言，我们提供了一个有原则且通用的框架，利用组合机制在更贴近真实攻击能力的复杂威胁模型下进行鲁棒性认证。

</details>


### [141] [MMD-Balls as Credal Sets: A PAC-Bayesian Framework for Epistemic Uncertainty in Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21783)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个PAC-贝叶斯框架，通过最大均值差异(MMD)参数化分布偏移下的泛化界，并将MMD球解释为Walley不精确概率理论中的credal集，从而分离认知不确定性与偶然不确定性，为测试时自适应(TTA)提供形式化的可靠性保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有TTA方法在分布偏移下能提升模型性能，但缺乏将偏移程度与预测可靠性联系起来的形式化理论保证。

Method: (i) 在RKHS-Lipschitz损失假设下，建立含MMD依赖偏移罚项的PAC-贝叶斯界；(ii) 通过MMD浓度不等式导出有限样本版本；(iii) 在credal集上给出均匀最坏情况风险界，并分解为下-上风险；(iv) 推导测地线保持界，解释核引导自适应为何保护局部特征几何。

Result: 建立了四个主要理论结果：MMD依赖的PAC-贝叶斯泛化界、有限样本版本、credal集上的均匀最坏情况风险界及上下风险分解、测地线保持界。credal集解释实现了认知不确定性与偶然不确定性的分离。

Conclusion: 该框架为TTA何时值得执行提供了原则性决策准则，通过将MMD球解释为credal集，有效分离了认知不确定性与偶然不确定性。

Original Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) methods improve model performance under distribution shift but lack formal guarantees connecting shift magnitude to prediction reliability. We develop a PAC-Bayesian framework yielding generalization bounds explicitly parameterized by the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between source and target distributions. Our principal contribution is interpreting MMD-balls around the source distribution as credal sets in Walley's imprecise probability theory, yielding natural epistemic uncertainty quantification. We establish: (i) a PAC-Bayesian bound with an MMD-dependent shift penalty under an RKHS-Lipschitz loss assumption; (ii) a finite-sample version via MMD concentration; (iii) a uniform worst-case risk bound over all distributions in the credal set, with a lower-upper risk decomposition; and (iv) geodesic preservation bounds explaining why kernel-guided adaptation protects local feature geometry. The credal set interpretation separates epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty and provides a principled decision criterion for when adaptation is warranted.

中文摘要翻译: 测试时自适应（TTA）方法在分布偏移下能提升模型性能，但缺乏将偏移程度与预测可靠性联系起来的形式化保证。我们开发了一个PAC-贝叶斯框架，其泛化界显式地以源分布与目标分布之间的最大均值差异（MMD）为参数。我们的主要贡献是将源分布周围的MMD球解释为Walley不精确概率理论中的credal集，从而自然地实现认知不确定性量化。我们建立了：（i）在RKHS-Lipschitz损失假设下，含MMD依赖偏移罚项的PAC-贝叶斯界；（ii）通过MMD浓度不等式得到的有限样本版本；（iii）credal集上所有分布的均匀最坏情况风险界，并具有下-上风险分解；（iv）测地线保持界，解释了为何核引导自适应能保护局部特征几何。基于credal集的解释分离了认知不确定性与偶然不确定性，并为何时自适应是合理的提供了原则性决策准则。

</details>


### [142] [stable-worldmodel: A Platform for Reproducible World Modeling Research and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21800)
*Lucas Maes,Quentin Le Lidec,Luiz Facury,Nassim Massaudi,Ayush Chaurasia,Francesco Capuano,Richard Gao,Taj Gillin,Dan Haramati,Damien Scieur,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 stable-worldmodel (swm)，一个用于标准化和可复现世界模型研究的开源平台，通过高性能数据层、标准化基线实现和可控泛化基准，解决当前世界模型研究中代码库碎片化、数据加载缓慢和缺乏统一评估标准的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前世界模型研究存在代码库碎片化、数据管线不统一、评估协议各异等问题，严重阻碍了研究的可复现性和公平比较；此外，脆弱的单次性代码库、缓慢的视频数据加载以及缺乏标准化泛化基准是三个关键瓶颈。

Method: 开发了 stable-worldmodel (swm) 开源平台，包含：(1) 基于 Lance 的高性能数据层，原生支持 MP4、HDF5 和 LeRobot 数据集并提供格式转换工具；(2) 现代世界模型基线和规划求解器的干净、充分测试的实现；(3) 扩展了视觉、几何和物理可控变化因素的广泛环境与任务套件，用于动力学理解、控制性能、表征质量和分布外泛化的系统性仿真评估。

Result: 通过统一完整的管线到一个单一可扩展框架下，swm 显著降低了研究开销，加速了向可靠世界模型的可信进展。

Conclusion: swm 通过提供标准化、可复现的研究与评估平台，有效解决了当前世界模型研究中的碎片化和可复现性问题，推动了该领域的可信进展。

Original Abstract: World models are central to building agents that can reason, plan, and generalize beyond their training data. However, research on world models is currently fragmented, with disparate codebases, data pipelines, and evaluation protocols hindering reproducibility and fair comparison. Current practice is further limited by three key bottlenecks: fragile one-off codebases, slow video data loading, and the lack of standardized generalization benchmarks. We present stable-worldmodel (swm), an open-source platform for standardized and reproducible world modeling research and evaluation. It delivers (1) a high-performance Lance-based data layer with native support and conversion tools for MP4, HDF5, and LeRobot datasets, (2) clean, well-tested implementations of modern world model baselines and planning solvers, and (3) a broad suite of environments and tasks extended with controllable visual, geometric, and physical factors of variation for systematic in-silico evaluation of dynamics understanding, control performance, representation quality, and out-of-distribution generalization. By unifying the full pipeline under a single, scalable framework, \texttt{swm} dramatically reduces research overhead and accelerates trustworthy progress toward reliable world models.

中文摘要翻译: 世界模型对于构建能够推理、规划并在训练数据之外进行泛化的智能体至关重要。然而，当前世界模型的研究存在碎片化问题，不同的代码库、数据管线和评估协议阻碍了可复现性和公平比较。目前的实践进一步受到三个关键瓶颈的限制：脆弱的单次性代码库、缓慢的视频数据加载以及缺乏标准化的泛化基准。我们提出了 stable-worldmodel (swm)，一个用于标准化和可复现的世界模型研究与评估的开源平台。该平台提供：(1) 基于 Lance 的高性能数据层，原生支持 MP4、HDF5 和 LeRobot 数据集并提供格式转换工具；(2) 现代世界模型基线和规划求解器的干净、充分测试的实现；(3) 广泛的环境和任务套件，扩展了可控的视觉、几何和物理变化因素，用于动力学理解、控制性能、表征质量和分布外泛化的系统性仿真评估。通过将完整的管线统一到一个单一可扩展框架下，swm 显著降低了研究开销，并加速了向可靠世界模型的可信进展。

</details>


### [143] [Why Semantic Entropy Fails: Geometry-Aware and Calibrated Uncertainty for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21801)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Kaiwen Shi,Han Bao,Zehong Wang,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何感知校准策略优化（GCPO）框架，通过几何感知度量捕捉语义分歧，并结合基于奖励的校准来对齐不确定性与学习信号强度，从而解决当前基于熵的不确定性估计器在梯度方差和学习信号质量表征中的各向异性缺口和校准缺口问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无批评模型利用响应级不确定性信号调节GRPO等群体优化方法，但其经验成功不稳定且对优化动态的影响不明确。论文旨在提供首个将不确定性信号解释为表征和调节梯度方差及学习信号质量机制的原则性公式。

Method: 提出几何感知校准策略优化（GCPO）框架，集成几何感知度量以捕捉语义分歧，并结合基于奖励的校准将不确定性与学习信号强度对齐，从而弥合各向异性缺口和校准缺口。

Result: 实验表明，GCPO能更准确地追踪梯度变异性，并在多个基准上持续提升后训练性能。

Conclusion: 设计符合优化动态的不确定性信号对于实现鲁棒的后训练至关重要，本文为这一方向提供了原则性的视角。

Original Abstract: Post-training has become central to improving reasoning and alignment in large language models, where critic-free models enable scalable learning from model-generated outputs but lack principled mechanisms to distinguish informative from noisy signals. Recent approaches leverage response-level measures as uncertainty signals to regulate group-based optimization methods such as GRPO. Yet their empirical success remains unstable and unclear in how they influence optimization dynamics. In this paper, we provide, to our knowledge, the first principled formulation that interprets uncertainty signals as mechanisms for characterizing and regulating gradient variance and learning signal quality. Based on both empirical and theoretical analysis, we identify two critical gaps of current entropy-based estimators: The anisotropic gap and The calibration gap. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Geometric-aware Calibrated Policy Optimization (GCPO), a novel framework integrating geometry-aware measures to capture semantic disagreement with reward-based calibration to align uncertainty with learning signal strength. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that our approach more faithfully tracks gradient variability and consistently improves post-training performance. Our results highlight the importance of designing uncertainty signals that are aligned with optimization dynamics, offering a principled perspective for robust post-training.

中文摘要翻译: 后训练已成为提升大型语言模型推理和对齐能力的核心手段，其中无批评模型能够利用模型生成的输出进行可扩展学习，但缺乏区分信息性信号与噪声信号的原则性机制。近期方法采用响应级度量作为不确定性信号来调控基于群体的优化方法（如GRPO），但其经验成功仍不稳定，且对优化动态的影响尚不明确。本文首次提供了原则性公式，将不确定性信号解释为表征和调节梯度方差及学习信号质量的机制。基于实证与理论分析，我们识别了当前基于熵的估计器的两个关键缺陷：各向异性缺口和校准缺口。受此启发，我们提出了几何感知校准策略优化（GCPO），这是一个新颖的框架，集成几何感知度量以捕捉语义分歧，并通过基于奖励的校准将不确定性与学习信号强度对齐。在多个基准上的实验表明，我们的方法能更准确地追踪梯度变异性，并持续提升后训练性能。我们的结果强调了设计符合优化动态的不确定性信号的重要性，为鲁棒的后训练提供了原则性视角。

</details>


### [144] [Symbolic Density Estimation for Discrete Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21813)
*Ziwen Liu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出符号密度估计（SDE）框架，通过组合初等解析运算自动发现离散概率分布的概率质量函数闭式表达式，并在基准数据集和真实应用中验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统离散分布族的构建依赖个例化的数学推导，进程缓慢，缺乏自动发现可解释分布的统一框架。

Method: 将领域特定的结构先验与进化搜索相结合，配合有效性感知的推理阶段，在结构化搜索空间内通过组合初等解析运算自动恢复概率质量函数的闭式表达式，并扩展到零膨胀和有限混合等更丰富的分布族。

Result: 该算法在基准数据集中恢复了所有分布族并获得了准确的参数估计；在真实数据应用中，识别出简洁可解释的混合模型，其拟合优度优于标准模型。

Conclusion: 符号密度估计是一种有效的无监督框架，能够自动发现可解释的离散分布，拓展了分布族的自动化构建能力。

Original Abstract: Discrete probability laws underpin statistical modeling, yet the catalog of interpretable distributions has expanded only gradually through centuries of case-by-case mathematical derivations. We introduce symbolic density estimation (SDE), an unsupervised framework that automatically recovers closed-form probability mass functions by composing elementary analytic operations within a structured search space. Our method integrates domain-specific structural priors with evolutionary search and a validity-aware inference stage, and it extends to richer distribution families such as zero inflation and finite mixtures. To support systematic evaluation and future research, we contribute a benchmark dataset spanning a broad collection of commonly used discrete distributions. The proposed algorithm recovers all benchmark families with accurate parameter estimates. A real data application shows that it identifies concise and interpretable mixture models that improve goodness-of-fit over standard models.

中文摘要翻译: 离散概率律是统计建模的基础，然而可解释分布族的目录历经数个世纪的逐案例数学推导才得以逐步扩展。本文提出符号密度估计（SDE），一种无监督框架，通过在结构化搜索空间内组合初等解析运算，自动恢复概率质量函数的闭式表达式。我们的方法将领域特定的结构先验与进化搜索及有效性感知的推理阶段相结合，并可扩展到更丰富的分布族，如零膨胀和有限混合。为了支持系统性评估和未来研究，我们提供了一个涵盖广泛常用离散分布的基准数据集。所提出的算法恢复了所有基准分布族，参数估计准确。真实数据应用表明，该方法能够识别出简洁且可解释的混合模型，其拟合优度优于标准模型。

</details>


### [145] [Beyond Scalar Objectives: Expert-Feedback-Driven Autonomous Experimentation for Scientific Discovery at the Nanoscale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21820)
*Ralph Bulanadi,Jefferey Baxter,Arpan Biswas,Hiroshi Funakubo,Dennis Meier,Jan Schultheiß,Rama Vasudevan,Yongtao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度核成对学习（DKPL），一种将人类专家知识融入主动学习循环的自主显微实验方法，无需预设标量描述符，通过专家直接评估实验输出并学习潜在效用函数来指导实验，在纳米结构学习和铁电畴壁分析中表现出色。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于贝叶斯优化的自主实验框架依赖预设标量描述符来指导实验，但确定合适的标量描述符往往具有挑战性，且可能无法捕捉跨学科专家才能识别的细微但重要的科学现象。为此，需要一种能够将人类专业知识和跨学科科学知识融入主动学习循环的新方法。

Method: 提出深度核成对学习（Deep-Kernel Pairwise Learning, DKPL），该方法不依赖显式标量目标，而是允许专家利用跨学科知识直接评估哪个实验输出更有前景，然后从这些专家判断中学习一个潜在效用函数，用于指导后续的自主显微实验。

Result: 在已知真实标签的实验模型数据集上，DKPL能够学习物理上有意义的纳米结构，同时有效优先选择高信息测量区域。在铁电畴壁分析中，DKPL能够区分铋铁氧体中高和低特征畴壁角，并能发现锰酸铒中头对头和尾对尾的畴壁特征。

Conclusion: DKPL建立了一种将专家知识整合到自主显微实验中的方法，展示了迈向专家引导的自动驾驶实验室的路径，能够解决超出标量度量驱动学习限制的科学问题。

Original Abstract: Self-driving laboratories or autonomous experimentation are emerging as transformative platforms for accelerating scientific discovery. Bayesian optimization (BO) is among the most widely used machine learning frameworks for these purposes, but these BO-based frameworks rely on predefined scalar descriptors to guide experimentation. In many situations, the determination of an appropriate scalar descriptor can be challenging, and may fail to capture subtle yet scientifically important phenomena apparent to experts with interdisciplinary insight. To overcome this limitation, here we develop deep-kernel pairwise learning (DKPL), an approach for autonomous microscopy experiments which incorporates human expertise and interdisciplinary scientific knowledge into an active learning loop. Instead of relying on explicit scalar objectives, DKPL enables experts to directly evaluate which experimental output is more promising using interdisciplinary knowledge. DKPL then learns a latent utility function from these expert judgements to guide subsequent autonomous microscopy experiments. We demonstrate DKPL's performance in learning physically meaningful nanoscale structures while effectively prioritizing high-information measurement regions using an experimental model dataset with known ground truth. We further apply DKPL to analyze the character of ferroelectric domain walls, where we find DKPL capable of distinguishing between high and low characteristic domain-wall angles in bismuth ferrite, and able to discover both head-to-head and tail-to-tail domain-wall character in erbium manganite. This development establishes an approach to integrate expert knowledge into autonomous microscopy experiments and demonstrates a pathway toward expert-guided self-driving laboratories capable of addressing scientific problems beyond the limits of scalar-metrics-driven learning.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶实验室或自主实验正成为加速科学发现的变革性平台。贝叶斯优化（BO）是最广泛使用的机器学习框架之一，但这些基于BO的框架依赖预定义的标量描述符来指导实验。在许多情况下，确定合适的标量描述符具有挑战性，且可能无法捕捉跨学科专家才能识别的细微但重要的科学现象。为克服这一局限，本文开发了深度核成对学习（DKPL），一种将人类专业知识和跨学科科学知识融入主动学习循环的自主显微实验方法。DKPL不依赖显式标量目标，而是允许专家利用跨学科知识直接评估哪个实验输出更有前景，然后从这些专家判断中学习潜在效用函数，以指导后续自主显微实验。我们在已知真实标签的实验模型数据集上展示了DKPL在学习物理上有意义的纳米结构方面的性能，同时有效优先选择高信息测量区域。我们进一步将DKPL应用于分析铁电畴壁的特征，发现DKPL能够区分铋铁氧体中高和低特征畴壁角，并能发现锰酸铒中头对头和尾对尾的畴壁特征。这一发展建立了一种将专家知识整合到自主显微实验中的方法，展示了通向专家引导的自动驾驶实验室的路径，能够解决超出标量度量驱动学习限制的科学问题。

</details>


### [146] [On-Policy Consistency Training Improves LLM Safety with Minimal Capability Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21834)
*Andy Han,Kristina Fujimoto,Avidan Shah,Kiet Nguyen,Kai Xu,Chen Yueh-Han,Ilia Sucholutsky,Rico Angell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线一致性训练（OPCT），通过在模型自身响应上计算一致性目标，显著优于传统的离线SFT一致性训练方法，在减少谄媚、防御越狱和提升安全感知三个安全维度上表现更佳，且避免了能力回退。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的一致性训练方法通过离线生成监督信号并使用监督微调（SFT）来更新模型，导致模型仅记忆训练分布的表面形式，泛化能力差且能力回退严重。需要一种能更好地泛化到训练分布之外的一致性训练方法。

Method: 提出在线一致性训练（OPCT），其目标函数在模型自身对提示的响应上计算，以对应对比提示为条件进行自我监督，替代传统的离线SFT方案。

Result: 在三个模型族上，OPCT在所有安全指标上均优于SFT：谄媚率从15.4%降至8.1%（SFT为11.2%）；自适应目标攻击下越狱防御成功率接近99%（SFT平均87%）；安全感知上两项优于SFT一项持平；避免了SFT导致的MATH-500上28个百分点的能力下降。

Conclusion: 一致性训练最好以OPCT而非SFT形式实现，尤其是在需要超越训练分布进行泛化的场景下。

Original Abstract: Aligned models can misbehave in several ways: they are often sycophantic, fall victim to jailbreaks, or fail to include appropriate safety warnings. Consistency training is a promising new alignment paradigm to mitigate such failures by training invariants into the model using contrastive input pairs. Existing consistency training procedures generate the supervision signal once, offline, and use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to update the model. Unfortunately, the resulting models tend to merely memorize the surface forms of the training distribution and thus generalize poorly and regress in their capabilities. We introduce On-Policy Consistency Training (OPCT), a new consistency training approach where the objective is computed over the model's own responses to prompts, supervised by itself conditioned on corresponding contrastive prompts. We evaluate OPCT on three safety axes: sycophancy, jailbreaking, and safety awareness. Across three model families, OPCT outperforms its SFT counterpart on all safety desiderata. It nearly halves the sycophancy rate relative to baseline (8.1% vs. 15.4%, compared to 11.2% for SFT). Under an adaptive per-target attacker, OPCT holds jailbreak defense success near 99% on held-out jailbreak behaviors, whereas SFT achieves 87% on average. On safety awareness, OPCT outperforms SFT in two out of three models, and matches it on the other. OPCT also largely avoids the capability regressions that SFT induces, such as a 28-point drop on MATH-500. Our results suggest that consistency training is best implemented as OPCT rather than as SFT, especially when generalization beyond the training distribution is desired.

中文摘要翻译: 对齐后的模型可能在多个方面出现不良行为：它们常常表现出谄媚倾向、容易受到越狱攻击，或未能给出适当的安全警告。一致性训练是一种有前景的新兴对齐范式，通过使用对比输入对训练模型的不变性来缓解此类问题。现有的一致性训练方法离线生成一次监督信号，并使用监督微调（SFT）来更新模型。然而，由此得到的模型往往仅仅记住了训练分布的表面形式，导致泛化能力差且能力退化。我们提出了在线一致性训练（OPCT），这是一种新的一致性训练方法，其目标函数在模型自身对提示的响应上计算，并以对应的对比提示为条件进行自我监督。我们在三个安全维度上评估OPCT：谄媚、越狱和安全感知。在三个模型族上，OPCT在所有安全指标上均优于其SFT对应方法。它将谄媚率相对于基线几乎减半（8.1%对比15.4%，而SFT为11.2%）。在自适应逐目标攻击下，OPCT在未见过的越狱行为上保持了接近99%的越狱防御成功率，而SFT平均达到87%。在安全感知方面，OPCT在三个模型中的两个上优于SFT，在另一个上与之持平。OPCT在很大程度上也避免了SFT所带来的能力回退，例如MATH-500上的28个百分点的下降。我们的结果表明，一致性训练最好以OPCT而非SFT的形式实现，尤其是在需要超越训练分布进行泛化的情况下。

</details>


### [147] [Geometry-Adaptive Explainer for Faithful Dictionary-Based Interpretability under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21849)
*Sungjun Lim,Heedong Kim,Andrew Lee,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现分布偏移会导致模型活跃子空间旋转，使得基于ID训练的字典解释器与OOD活跃子空间错位，造成忠实度下降。为此提出GAE方法，仅需无标注OOD激活即可重新对齐字典，无需梯度更新，理论证明其误差受二阶矩偏移的二次界约束，实验表明GAE在因果忠实度上匹配甚至超越所有基于训练的基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于字典的解释器（如稀疏自编码器、Transcoder）在分布内数据上表现良好，但在分布偏移（OOD）下的忠实度缺乏系统研究。本文旨在揭示OOD下解释器忠实度退化的根本原因并提出修复方法。

Method: 提出几何自适应解释器（Geometry-Adaptive Explainer, GAE）：通过将ID字典与OOD活跃子空间重新对齐，同时保留原始特征结构。该方法仅需无标注的OOD激活，无需梯度更新。理论上证明了GAE的改进效果，其超额损失被二阶矩偏移的二次界所约束。

Result: 在多个模型和多种OOD设置下，GAE在因果忠实度指标上匹配甚至超越所有基于训练的基线方法。理论分析表明，忠实度缺口（faithfulness gap）——ID字典与OOD活跃子空间之间的几何距离——控制了OOD忠实度退化程度。

Conclusion: 分布偏移导致的字典-子空间错位是解释器OOD忠实度退化的核心原因，GAE通过几何对齐有效缓解了该问题，无需重新训练即可实现优越的OOD忠实度。

Original Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to explain a model's behavior by identifying causally responsible internal structures. Dictionary-based explainers such as sparse autoencoders and transcoders are a primary tool, but their faithfulness under out-of-distribution (OOD) shift has received little systematic attention. We show that distribution shift rotates the subspace that the model actively uses, misaligning the explainer's dictionary trained on in-distribution (ID) activations. We formalize this misalignment as the faithfulness gap, a geometric distance between the ID dictionary and the OOD-active subspace, and show that it controls OOD faithfulness degradation. To reduce this gap, we propose the Geometry-Adaptive Explainer (GAE), which realigns the explainer's dictionary with the OOD-active subspace while preserving the original feature structure. This requires only unlabeled OOD activations and no gradient updates. We prove that GAE improves over the unadapted ID explainer, with excess loss bounded quadratically by the second-moment shift. Empirically, GAE even matches or surpasses all training-based baselines in causal faithfulness across multiple models and OOD settings.

中文摘要翻译: 机械可解释性旨在通过识别因果上负责的内部结构来解释模型的行为。基于字典的解释器（如稀疏自编码器和Transcoder）是主要工具，但它们在分布外（OOD）偏移下的忠实度鲜有系统性的关注。我们发现分布偏移会旋转模型实际使用的子空间，导致在分布内（ID）激活上训练的解释器字典与OOD活跃子空间错位。我们将这种错位形式化为忠实度缺口，即ID字典与OOD活跃子空间之间的几何距离，并证明它控制了OOD忠实度的退化程度。为缩小这一缺口，我们提出几何自适应解释器（Geometry-Adaptive Explainer, GAE），它能在保留原始特征结构的同时，将解释器字典与OOD活跃子空间重新对齐。该方法仅需无标注的OOD激活，无需梯度更新。我们证明GAE优于未适应的ID解释器，其超额损失被二阶矩偏移的二次界所约束。实验结果表明，在多个模型和多种OOD设置下，GAE在因果忠实度上匹配甚至超越所有基于训练的基线方法。

</details>


### [148] [The Illusion of Reasoning: Exposing Evasive Data Contamination in LLMs via Zero-CoT Truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21856)
*Yifan Lan,Yuanpu Cao,Hanyu Wang,Lu Lin,Jinghui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Zero-CoT Probe (ZCP)，一种通过截断链式思维过程来暴露模型记忆痕迹的黑盒数据污染检测方法，能有效检测直接和间接（如释义）的数据污染。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务中表现出色，但数据污染（尤其是恶意发布者通过释义等规避手段进行的间接污染）严重损害了评估的客观性，现有方法难以可靠检测这类隐蔽污染。

Method: 发现模型生成的推理步骤会主动掩盖其记忆行为；ZCP通过截断整个CoT过程（零-CoT）来暴露潜在的捷径映射，并通过对比原始基准和同构扰动参考数据集上的零-CoT性能，分离记忆与真正推理能力；引入Contamination Confidence指标量化污染的可能性和严重性。

Result: 在已知污染模型和专门微调的污染模型上的大量实验表明，ZCP能够稳健地检测直接和间接（规避性）的数据污染。

Conclusion: ZCP通过主动截断推理过程，有效暴露了模型的记忆行为，是一种可检测直接与间接数据污染的鲁棒黑盒方法。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive reasoning abilities across a wide range of tasks, but data contamination undermines the objective evaluation of these capabilities. This problem is further exacerbated by malicious model publishers who use evasive, or indirect, contamination strategies, such as paraphrasing benchmark data to evade existing detection methods and artificially boost leaderboard performance. Current approaches struggle to reliably detect such stealthy contamination. In this work, we uncover a critical phenomenon: a model's generated reasoning steps actively mask its underlying memorization. Inspired by this, we propose the Zero-CoT Probe (ZCP), a novel black-box detection method that deliberately truncates the entire Chain-of-Thought (CoT) process to expose latent shortcut mappings. To further isolate memorization from the model's intrinsic problem-solving capabilities, ZCP compares the model's zero-CoT performance on the original benchmark against an isomorphically perturbed reference dataset. Furthermore, we introduce Contamination Confidence, a metric that quantifies both the likelihood and severity of contamination, moving beyond simple binary classifications. Extensive experiments on both previously identified contaminated models and specially fine-tuned contaminated models demonstrate that ZCP robustly detects both direct and evasive data contamination. The code for ZCP is accessible at https://github.com/Yifan-Lan/zero-cot-probe.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLMs）在广泛的任务中展现出令人印象深刻的推理能力，但数据污染削弱了对其能力的客观评估。恶意模型发布者使用规避性或间接的污染策略（例如对基准数据进行释义以逃避现有检测方法并人为提升排行榜性能）进一步加剧了这一问题。当前的方法难以可靠地检测此类隐蔽污染。在这项工作中，我们发现了一个关键现象：模型生成的推理步骤会主动掩盖其潜在的记忆行为。受此启发，我们提出了Zero-CoT Probe (ZCP)，一种新颖的黑盒检测方法，它通过故意截断整个思维链（Chain-of-Thought, CoT）过程来暴露潜在的捷径映射。为了进一步将记忆能力与模型内在的问题求解能力分离开来，ZCP将模型在原始基准上的零-CoT性能与在一个同构扰动的参考数据集上的性能进行对比。此外，我们引入了污染置信度（Contamination Confidence），一个能够量化污染可能性与严重程度的度量指标，超越了简单的二元分类。在先前已识别的污染模型和专门微调的污染模型上进行的大量实验表明，ZCP能够稳健地检测直接和规避性的数据污染。ZCP的代码可在 https://github.com/Yifan-Lan/zero-cot-probe 获取。

</details>


### [149] [When to Switch, Not Just What: Transition Quality Prediction in Clash Royale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21868)
*Heeyun Heo,Huy Kang Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现竞技游戏中玩家在连败后频繁切换策略反而降低胜率，指出传统推荐系统忽略了切换成本与个体差异（零切换成本假设），并提出TQP三阶段流水线（Who→When→What）进行策略推荐，同时引入新评估指标SwitchGap，在5.4%推荐率下实现+10.4个百分点的SwitchGap提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有推荐系统仅评估策略的期望质量，忽略了切换行为本身带来的成本以及玩家个体在切换倾向上的差异。作者将这一隐含前提称为"零切换成本假设"，并试图修正这一缺陷。

Method: 将策略推荐重构为过渡级决策问题，提出TQP（Transition Quality Predictor）三阶段流水线：1) PersonaGate：抑制对策略一致性本身与高胜率相关玩家的推荐；2) TimingGate：利用子类型和状态匹配的基线来控制自然胜率恢复，识别切换可能带来净收益的时机；3) ScoreFusion：结合可采纳性信号与预测的过渡质量（delta胜率）对候选策略进行排序。同时提出新评估指标SwitchGap，不将玩家选择视为最优真实标签。

Result: 完整流水线在5.4%推荐率下实现了+10.4个百分点的SwitchGap；连败触发的切换者虽然是表现最差的群体，但从子类型条件化的指导中获益最多。

Conclusion: 在竞技游戏策略推荐中必须考虑切换成本与个体差异，所提出的TQP流水线能有效识别真正有益的切换时机并推荐合适策略，尤其对低胜率、易切换的玩家群体帮助最大。

Original Abstract: In competitive games, players frequently switch strategies after losing streaks, yet our analysis of 926,334 match records from 34,619 Clash Royale players reveals a counterintuitive pattern: switching frequency is inversely associated with the win rate, with effects that vary substantially across players and situational contexts. We attribute this to a limitation common in many prior recommendation systems, which evaluate strategies by expected quality while overlooking the behavioral cost of switching and individual differences in switching propensity.
